5. AUTH TESTS (BASIC)
=====================

Test:

Login success

Invalid credentials

Missing fields

Unauthorized protected route

Token validation

Example cases:

valid email/password → success

wrong password → fail

missing token → unauthorized

expired token → unauthorized

Keep only basic security validation.

==================================================
6. TRANSFER MONEY TESTS (IMPORTANT)
===================================

Test core BFSI flow.

Cases:

successful transfer

insufficient balance

invalid amount

same account transfer blocked

missing required field

invalid beneficiary

Validate:

transaction created

balance updated

proper success response

failed transfer response

Do NOT deeply test internal implementation.

Test behavior only.

==================================================
7. BILL PAYMENT TESTS
=====================

Cases:

missing required fields

Validate:

balance deduction

basic validation only.

==================================================
8. BENEFICIARY TESTS

Test:

response success

duplicate prevention

==================================================
9. ACTIVITY LOG TESTS
=====================

Basic API testing only.

Cases:

fetch activity list

pagination works

authenticated access

empty response handling

Do NOT deeply test filtering logic.

Keep basic.

==================================================
10. MIDDLEWARE TESTS
====================

Test:

JWT authentication middleware

protected routes

unauthorized request

invalid token

missing token

Keep simple.

==================================================
11. DATABASE APPROACH
=====================

Keep DB testing simple.

DO NOT introduce advanced in-memory DB setup unless necessary.

Prefer:

mock service behavior

simple test-friendly approach

Avoid complex DB orchestration.

==================================================
12. TEST STYLE
==============

Use readable structure.

Example style:

describe()

it()

expect()

Clear naming.

Readable tests.

No deeply nested test cases.

==================================================
13. WHAT TO AVOID
=================

DO NOT:

aim for high coverage

over-mock everything

write advanced integration tests

test every edge case

test private methods

test internal implementation details

Only test behavior.

Add scripts:

Ensure tests run cleanly.
